(2) The Upstash Open Source Program ($1,000/mo credits) is currently closed to new applications per the official page. (3) No official education/student program exists: /education 404s, the Redis FAQ has no education entry; third-party 'student deal' pages (Modelverse, ToolBridge) merely describe the general free tier and cite outdated figures (10,000 commands/day, 256 MB) vs today's official 500K commands/month, 250 MB. (4) A widely circulated 'Upstash $500 startup credits' claim (SaaSOffers) has no corresponding official page — no /startups, no startup section in pricing or FAQs. (5) Free Redis numbers changed from the old 10K/day+256MB form to 500K/month+250MB with 10 free databases; Vector is region-capped (N. Virginia, Ireland, Iowa) and Box runs only in AWS us-east-1.

### Weaviate Cloud Free Tier

- **Free Plan ("Always free" managed Weaviate cluster)** (high) — Forever $0/mo. Limits verified on the live pricing page 2026-10-22: 1 cluster per user (shared cluster on AWS, 2 regions), 100,000 objects, 1 GB memory, 10 GB disk, 1 collection (up to 3 tenants). Includes hybrid search, vector compression, multi-tenancy, RBAC. Excludes HA/replication, flexible index types, SSO/SAML, backup retention, PrivateLink, encrypted volumes, metrics. Uptime SLA: best effort (no %). Support: basic/email only. Upgrade to paid anytime (Flex from $45/mo, Premium shared from ~$400/mo, dedicated contract) with no migration.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to anyone; NO credit card required (page states "No credit card required"); no .edu email or startup requirement; 1 cluster per user; deployment limited to 2 regions (shared AWS cluster)
  - *How to claim:* Click "Start free" at weaviate.io/pricing -> sign up at console.weaviate.cloud (routed via /go/console); quickstart at docs.weaviate.io/cloud/quickstart; no card or approval needed
  - *Expiry:* None - "Forever $0/mo", "no credit card or time expiration" (per the 2026-06-17 launch post); persists indefinitely as long as the cluster is in use; can upgrade to paid at any time
  - *Gotchas:* Data transfer is free only for a "promotional period" - footnote says Weaviate may introduce egress charges in the future with advance notice; page defines no hard stop/overage mechanism for the Free plan (implicit path is upgrading to Flex/Premium); shared cluster means no uptime SLA; not suitable for production HA
  - *Source:* https://weaviate.io/pricing
- **Free AI services on the Free plan: Weaviate Embeddings 2,000 req/day; Query Agent 1,000 req/mo; Engram "Always free"** (high) — Free plan AI-service allowances: Weaviate Embeddings 2,000 requests/day; Query Agent 1,000 requests/month (paid plans: "Free to try" 1,000 req/mo, then $30/organization per month with 4,000 req included, then usage-based); Engram (managed agent-memory layer) labeled "Always free" with no itemized quota on the pricing page. Part of the 2026-06-17 rollout making the entire suite free to start. Embedding models otherwise billed by usage at $0.025 / $0.040 / $0.065 per 1M tokens across three model tiers.
  - *Eligibility:* Same as Free plan: anyone, no credit card, 1 cluster per user; Query Agent/Engram are cloud-only services (not runnable via local Docker)
  - *How to claim:* Same account as Free plan; enable services in the Weaviate Cloud console - no separate enrollment
  - *Expiry:* Indefinite while on the Free plan; overages on paid plans are usage-based
  - *Gotchas:* Engram free-tier quota is not specified anywhere on the page; exceeding embedding/Query Agent caps leads to usage-based billing or upgrade on paid plans; "Free to try" on Query Agent for paid tiers is a standing allowance, not a time-boxed trial
  - *Source:* https://weaviate.io/pricing
- **No trial-credit program (time-limited sandbox sunset, replaced by permanent free tier)** (medium) — No trial credits, 14/30-day trial, or credit packs exist on the official site as of 2026-10-22. The 2026-06-17 launch post states managed Weaviate Cloud previously offered only "a time-limited sandbox," which was replaced by the permanent free tier ("free as long as it's useful to you" vs "free until the clock runs out").
  - *Eligibility:* n/a - absence of offer
  - *How to claim:* n/a
  - *Expiry:* Time-limited sandbox model retired effective 2026-06-17, replaced by the permanent Free plan
  - *Gotchas:* Absence verified by direct page inspection only (pricing, homepage, blog index pages 1-2, 404 on /startup-program); the shared session web-search budget was exhausted, so third-party/press confirmation of older trial programs was not possible
  - *Source:* https://weaviate.io/blog/weaviate-free-tier
- **No startup program and no education/student license found** (medium) — weaviate.io/startup-program returns HTTP 404; homepage, pricing, and blog index (2025-2026 entries visible) contain no startup program, startup credits/grants, or education/academic pricing tier. Closest items: Weaviate Hero Program (weaviate.io/community) - community contribution program rewarding active community members with "Cool Merch, Books & Credits" plus workshops and event access; credits have no stated dollar value, cap, or deadline, and it is contribution-based, not status-based (not aimed at students or startups). Weaviate Academy (academy.weaviate.io) is free learning content (courses/resources), not a license. The Weaviate database is open source and free to self-host on your own infrastructure - the only genuinely unrestricted "education-grade" free path.
  - *Eligibility:* No .edu requirement or startup age/funding cap exists because no such program exists; Hero Program is open to any community contributor via a Google Form application
  - *How to claim:* No claim path for startup/education offers; Hero Program: apply via "Become a Hero" form (forms.gle/FHwhfPYwCVKkzw5h6)
  - *Expiry:* n/a
  - *Gotchas:* Hero Program "credits" are unquantified - do not treat as a free-credit offer; absence findings are limited to the official site (search engine verification unavailable this session); check Weaviate's social channels/blog for any program launched after the visible blog index
  - *Source:* https://weaviate.io/community
- **2025–26 change:** 2025-2026 changes: (1) 2026-06-17: Weaviate sunsetted its time-limited sandbox model for managed Weaviate Cloud and launched the permanent free tier spanning the whole product suite (managed Database + Query Agent + Engram), free without credit card or time expiration (blog: "Weaviate Cloud is now free to start"). (2) Price-book revision effective 1 September 2026 (noted on the pricing page): RQ-4 compression added as its own rate tier between RQ-1 and RQ-8, and backup rates restructured to a common base with regional adjustments - affects paid Flex/Premium plans only, Free plan unchanged. (3) Data transfer is free on all plans only for a "promotional period"; Weaviate reserves the right to charge for egress in the future with advance notice. No evidence was found of any sunset of a startup program or education license (none appear to exist; /startup-program is a 404). Caveat: verification was done by fetching official pages directly on 2026-10-22 (weaviate.io/pricing, /blog, /blog/page/2, /blog/weaviate-free-tier, /community, homepage; docs subpages now 404 - docs moved to docs.weaviate.io/cloud/quickstart); the session's web-search budget was exhausted, so third-party/press corroboration and deeper blog archive pages were not checked.

### Xata

- **Xata Open Source (self-hosted, free forever)** (high) — Xata's core Postgres branching platform is open source under Apache 2.0 (github.com/xataio). Self-hosting it on your own infrastructure is $0 platform fee and includes copy-on-write branching, scale-to-zero (wake-up ~10s), OpenEBS/Mayastor storage and community support. It does NOT include managed observability, SOC 2/GDPR compliance, multi-region, or dedicated support — you operate your own cluster. This is the only PERMANENT free path; there is no permanent free hosted/managed tier.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone; no .edu email, no startup age/funding cap, no credit card, no region restriction. Requires you to provision and operate your own infrastructure (Kubernetes + CloudNativePG + OpenEBS). Self-managed.
  - *How to claim:* Free forever, no trial clock, no CC required. Clone the xataio GitHub org and deploy on your own cluster to use the platform at $0.
  - *Expiry:* None (permanent, Apache 2.0 open source).
  - *Gotchas:* It is self-hosted, not a managed service: you carry all ops burden, no multi-region, no SOC 2/HIPAA compliance tooling, no 24/7 support, and wake-up is ~10s (vs ~1s on managed Xata Cloud). The managed convenience (SOC 2, multi-region, dedicated support) requires paid Xata Cloud or BYOC.
  - *Source:* https://xata.io/pricing
- **Xata Cloud 14-day free trial (managed)** (high) — The fully managed Xata Cloud (SOC 2/GDPR, multi-region on AWS+GCP) is offered as a 14-day free trial with NO credit card required during the trial. After the trial you must add a credit card to continue. Post-trial billing is pure usage: from $0.012/hr for a micro instance (<=2 vCPU, 1 GB RAM, ~$9/mo) up to $1.536/hr (32 vCPU, 128 GB), plus $0.28/GB/month storage billed by the minute. No per-branch or per-user fees; scale-to-zero means idle branches cost no compute.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone who signs up at console.xata.io; email required, no credit card during the 14-day trial, no .edu or startup proof. A credit card IS required to keep using it after the trial. Managed Xata Cloud currently runs on AWS us-east-1 and eu-central-1 (third-party-reported), so US/EU regions apply.
  - *How to claim:* Start the 14-day managed trial at console.xata.io; no card needed to begin. Add a card afterward to convert to pay-as-you-go.
  - *Expiry:* 14 days, then pay-as-you-go.
  - *Gotchas:* This is a trial, not a free tier: the clock runs and a card is mandatory after 14 days. After the trial you still pay for compute hours plus $0.28/GB/mo storage — a side-project database that wakes up frequently keeps accruing cost, and idle branches still incur the storage line. The 'no CC required' promise applies only to the trial window. The docs (n8n quickstart) confirm: 'To keep using Xata after the free trial, add a credit card in the billing system.'
  - *Source:* https://xata.io/pricing
- **Xata Cloud $100 onboarding / signup credit (unconfirmed)** (medium) — Several 2026 third-party pricing trackers and the open-source release coverage describe a $100 onboarding/signup credit applied during the first 14 days of Xata Cloud. This credit is NOT shown on the live xata.io/pricing page nor in the machine-readable pricing.md I read today (both only state a '14-day free trial', no dollar figure), so its current availability is unconfirmed.
  - *Eligibility:* Reported as tied to new Xata Cloud signups / the trial; no .edu or startup proof found (unverified); a credit card is required to continue past the trial.
  - *How to claim:* Reported as a '$100 onboarding credit (first 14 days)' by third-party trackers; not confirmed on the live official pricing page today. Verify via the signup flow or sales contact before relying on it.
  - *Expiry:* Reported as tied to the first 14 days / signup; unconfirmed.
  - *Gotchas:* Could not verify the amount against the live official pricing page or pricing.md today; the figure and whether it is still offered may have changed. Treat as unconfirmed. Corroborating third-party sources: srvrlss.io and dev.to coverage of the Apache 2.0 release both cite a '$100 signup credit' alongside the 14-day trial.
  - *Source:* https://valuehub.valuecore.ai/valuehub/campaigns/index_ventures/xata-io/pricing
- **Startup program / startup credits — not found** (medium) — No dedicated Xata startup program or startup-only credit exists on the official site, and Xata does not appear in 2026 free-startup-credits aggregators (e.g., StudentPerks startup-credits list). Xata's free-adjacent offers are generic to everyone (open-source self-host, 14-day trial, the reported $100 credit), not gated on startup age or a funding cap.
  - *Eligibility:* N/A — no official startup program found; therefore no startup age or funding cap, because the program does not exist. A sales contact (cal.com/team/xata/xata-sales) is the only bespoke path.
  - *How to claim:* No official startup program found as of 2026-10-22. Any discount for a startup would be via the generic 14-day trial (and the reported $100 credit) or a direct sales conversation.
  - *Expiry:* N/A (absence-based finding).
  - *Gotchas:* This is an absence finding, not a confirmed program. A third-party SaaSOffers page lists a generic 'free plan' as a startup deal, but that is an aggregator description, not an official Xata startup program. Confirm with Xata sales before assuming a startup offer exists.
  - *Source:* https://xata.io
- **Education license / student (.edu) offer — not found** (medium) — No Xata education license, .edu program, or student-specific offer was found on the official site or in 2025–2026 sources, and Xata is not listed in GitHub-Student-Developer-Pack-style aggregators. The only free paths available to a student are the same generic ones: open-source self-hosting (free, no CC) and the 14-day Xata Cloud trial.
  - *Eligibility:* N/A — no .edu email requirement because no education program was found; students are not treated differently from any other signer for the trial or OSS.
  - *How to claim:* No official education/student license found as of 2026-10-22. Students get the same generic 14-day trial and open-source self-host path as anyone; no .edu credential is required or requested.
  - *Expiry:* N/A (absence-based finding).
  - *Gotchas:* Absence finding, not a confirmed offer. Verify with xata.io or Xata sales before concluding that no education program exists.
  - *Source:* https://xata.io
- **2025–26 change:** Major 2025–2026 sunset/change: the permanent free hosted tier that students/startups relied on is GONE. Xata's original product was a hosted 'serverless data platform' (spreadsheet-style UI, REST/TypeScript data API, full-text search, file attachments over Postgres) that shipped a permanent free tier with 15 GB of storage, no cold starts and no pausing. Sunset timeline: (1) On January 10, 2025 Xata pulled the search and files APIs out of the free plan; (2) in May 2025 Xata announced a rebuilt product centered on vanilla, unmodified Postgres with copy-on-write branching and data anonymization (pgstream) — the old platform was briefly renamed 'Xata Lite' and now returns HTTP 410 Gone at lite.xata.io; (3) in late 2025 Xata open-sourced its core under Apache 2.0, and the permanent free hosted tier was replaced by 'self-host the open-source version' plus a 14-day Xata Cloud trial (with a reported $100 signup credit) and no permanent free cloud plan. Consequence: if you used the old Xata free tier for a managed database, it no longer exists — the only permanent-free option is self-hosted open source; the managed path is the 14-day trial then pay-as-you-go ($0.012/hr + $0.28/GB/mo) or BYOC (quote-based). Note: WebSearch budget was exhausted mid-task, so verification relied on the official pricing page + pricing.md + n8n docs read today plus multiple corroborating 2026 third-party trackers; the $100 credit could not be confirmed on the live official page.
